<article class="post" data-id="88123">
  <h1>Tokenizer Notes</h1>
  <p>Round-trip must be <em>exact</em> for &lt;code&gt; &amp; whitespace.</p>
  <ul>
    <li>tabs	preserved</li>
    <li>trailing spaces preserved   </li>
  </ul>
</article>
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<config xmlns:j="urn:juniper:tokenizer">
  <j:vocab size="36864" algorithm="utf8-byte-level-bpe"/>
  <j:special start="256" end="511"/>
</config>

  "objective_id": "obj-0007",

services:
  gateway:
    image: registry.example.com/gateway:1.8.2
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
    environment:
      LOG_LEVEL: info
      TIMEOUT_MS: "2500"
    depends_on: [redis, worker]

    build: ./worker
    deploy:
      replicas: 3
      resources:
        limits: { cpus: "0.50", memory: 256M }

<article class="post" data-id="88123">
  <h1>Tokenizer Notes</h1>
  <p>Round-trip must be <em>exact</em> for &lt;code&gt; &amp; whitespace.</p>

  <p>Round-trip must be <em>exact</em> for &lt;code&gt; &amp; whitespace.</p>
  <ul>
    <li>tabs	preserved</li>
    <li>trailing spaces preserved   </li>
  </ul>
</article>
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<config xmlns:j="urn:juniper:tokenizer">
  <j:vocab size="36864" algorithm="utf8-byte-level-bpe"/>
  <j:special start="256" end="511"/>
</config>

  <p>Round-trip must be <em>exact</em> for &lt;code&gt; &amp; whitespace.</p>
  <ul>
    <li>tabs	preserved</li>
    <li>trailing spaces preserved   </li>
  </ul>
</article>

<article class="post" data-id="88123">
  <h1>Tokenizer Notes</h1>
  <p>Round-trip must be <em>exact</em> for &lt;code&gt; &amp; whitespace.</p>
  <ul>
    <li>tabs	preserved</li>
    <li>trailing spaces preserved   </li>
  </ul>
</article>
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>

  <j:vocab size="36864" algorithm="utf8-byte-level-bpe"/>

</config>

    depends_on: [redis, worker]

  <h1>Tokenizer Notes</h1>
  <p>Round-trip must be <em>exact</em> for &lt;code&gt; &amp; whitespace.</p>
  <ul>
    <li>tabs	preserved</li>
    <li>trailing spaces preserved   </li>
  </ul>
</article>

<article class="post" data-id="88123">
  <h1>Tokenizer Notes</h1>
  <p>Round-trip must be <em>exact</em> for &lt;code&gt; &amp; whitespace.</p>
  <ul>
    <li>tabs	preserved</li>
    <li>trailing spaces preserved   </li>
  </ul>
</article>

    image: registry.example.com/gateway:1.8.2
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
    environment:
      LOG_LEVEL: info
      TIMEOUT_MS: "2500"
    depends_on: [redis, worker]
  worker:
    build: ./worker
    deploy:
      replicas: 3
      resources:
        limits: { cpus: "0.50", memory: 256M }
<article class="post" data-id="88123">
  <h1>Tokenizer Notes</h1>
  <p>Round-trip must be <em>exact</em> for &lt;code&gt; &amp; whitespace.</p>
  <ul>
    <li>tabs	preserved</li>
    <li>trailing spaces preserved   </li>
  </ul>
</article>

<article class="post" data-id="88123">
  <h1>Tokenizer Notes</h1>
  <p>Round-trip must be <em>exact</em> for &lt;code&gt; &amp; whitespace.</p>
  <ul>
    <li>tabs	preserved</li>

  <p>Round-trip must be <em>exact</em> for &lt;code&gt; &amp; whitespace.</p>
  <ul>
    <li>tabs	preserved</li>
    <li>trailing spaces preserved   </li>
  </ul>
</article>
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<config xmlns:j="urn:juniper:tokenizer">
  <j:vocab size="36864" algorithm="utf8-byte-level-bpe"/>
  <j:special start="256" end="511"/>
</config>
services:
  gateway:
    image: registry.example.com/gateway:1.8.2
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
    environment:
      LOG_LEVEL: info
      TIMEOUT_MS: "2500"
    depends_on: [redis, worker]
  worker:
    build: ./worker
    deploy:
      replicas: 3
      resources:
        limits: { cpus: "0.50", memory: 256M }
{
  "objective_id": "obj-0007",
  "status": "in_progress",

    {"id": 2, "action": "summarize", "done": false, "notes": "waiting on step 1"}

<article class="post" data-id="88123">
  <h1>Tokenizer Notes</h1>
  <p>Round-trip must be <em>exact</em> for &lt;code&gt; &amp; whitespace.</p>
  <ul>
    <li>tabs	preserved</li>
    <li>trailing spaces preserved   </li>
  </ul>
</article>

  <ul>
    <li>tabs	preserved</li>
    <li>trailing spaces preserved   </li>
  </ul>
</article>

<article class="post" data-id="88123">
  <h1>Tokenizer Notes</h1>
  <p>Round-trip must be <em>exact</em> for &lt;code&gt; &amp; whitespace.</p>
  <ul>
    <li>tabs	preserved</li>
    <li>trailing spaces preserved   </li>
  </ul>
